<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-3B658BC9-91F0-4BBD-9AD6-73E314CEF7D9'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which of these fractions are greater than one half?

$\dfrac{3}{5} \quad \dfrac{3}{7} \quad  \dfrac{5}{12} \quad \dfrac{8}{15} \quad  \dfrac{11}{20}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

A fraction will be bigger than $\dfrac{1}{2}$ if twice of its numerator is bigger than the denominator.

Using trial and error,


| Fraction  |$2 \times$ Numerator|         $> \dfrac{1}{2}$         |
|:-------:  |:------------------:|:-------------------------------: |
| $3, 5$    |  $3 \times 2 = 6$  |             Yes                  |
| $3, 7$    |  $3 \times 2 = 6$  |              No                  |
| $5, 12$   |  $5 \times 2 = 6$  |              No                  |
| $8, 15$   |  $8 \times 2 = 6$  |             Yes                  |
| $11, 15$  |  $11 \times 2 = 6$ |             Yes                  |


$ \dfrac{3}{5}, \dfrac{8}{15}, \dfrac{11}{15} $

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$ \dfrac{3}{5}, \dfrac{8}{15}, \dfrac{11}{15} $

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>A fraction will be bigger than $\dfrac{1}{2}$ if twice of its numerator is bigger than the denominator.

Using trial and error,


| Fraction  |$2 \times$ Numerator|         $> \dfrac{1}{2}$         |
|:-------:  |:------------------:|:-------------------------------: |
| $3, 5$    |  $3 \times 2 = 6$  |             Yes                  |
| $3, 7$    |  $3 \times 2 = 6$  |              No                  |
| $5, 12$   |  $5 \times 2 = 6$  |              No                  |
| $8, 15$   |  $8 \times 2 = 6$  |             Yes                  |
| $11, 15$  |  $11 \times 2 = 6$ |             Yes                  |


$ \dfrac{3}{5}, \dfrac{8}{15}, \dfrac{11}{15} $
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$ \dfrac{3}{5}, \dfrac{8}{15}, \dfrac{11}{15} $
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-70DEFCEB-7B9B-4701-99E3-3114A8B5C24C'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Find the missing number.

$513 +  + 130 = 764$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the missing number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
513 + a + 130 &= 764 \\\\
a + 643       &= 764 \\\\
a             &= 764 - 643 \\\\
a             &= 121
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$121$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the missing number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
513 + a + 130 &= 764 \\\\
a + 643       &= 764 \\\\
a             &= 764 - 643 \\\\
a             &= 121
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-9765422F-8002-4C2D-B4DF-7DA87D35A226'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$a * b$ means multiply the two numbers together and then subtract the larger.

So 

$
\begin{aligned}
4 * 6 &= 24 - 6 \\
          &= 18
\end{aligned}
$

Find the value of $8 * 3$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
8 * 3   &= (8 \times 3) - 8 \\\\
        &= 24 - 8 \\\\
        &= 16
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$16$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
8 * 3   &= (8 \times 3) - 8 \\\\
        &= 24 - 8 \\\\
        &= 16
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$16$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-1A2708F9-7326-46F8-A5AE-8365E91E007C'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Give a decimal number lying between $7.2$ and $7.3$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (7.2 + 7.3) \\\\
&= \dfrac {14.5} {2} \\\\
&= 7.25
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7.25$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (7.2 + 7.3) \\\\
&= \dfrac {14.5} {2} \\\\
&= 7.25
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7.25$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-100FBB17-3D32-4BB8-B59C-E8F8F8AC0D0E'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $4 \times (6.2 + 1.05)$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS
,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4 \times (6.2 + 1.05)  \\\\
&= 4 \times 7.25 \\\\
&= 29
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$29$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4 \times (6.2 + 1.05)  \\\\
&= 4 \times 7.25 \\\\
&= 29
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$29$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-437B376F-722B-4F93-B234-58C2F593FE92'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What fraction of an hour is $12 \ \text{minutes}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Fraction}    &= \dfrac {12} {60} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {12} {12 \times 5} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {12}} {\cancel {12} \times 5} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {5}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {5}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Fraction}    &= \dfrac {12} {60} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {12} {12 \times 5} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {12}} {\cancel {12} \times 5} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {5}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {1} {5}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FACB2640-E039-4BB9-9697-8C4685D190F2'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $8 \times (4.1 + 1.07)$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS
,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 8 \times (4.1 + 1.07)  \\\\
&= 8 \times 5.17 \\\\
&= 41.36
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$41.36$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 8 \times (4.1 + 1.07)  \\\\
&= 8 \times 5.17 \\\\
&= 41.36
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$41.36$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B9065A5E-7C52-4C0F-A700-FFA2337EB5BC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A parcel weighs $3.7\ \text{kg}$. What is its weight in $\text{grams}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  3.7 \ \text {kg} \\\\
&=  3.7 \times 1000 \ \text {g} \\\\
&=  3700 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3700 \ \text {g}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&=  3.7 \ \text {kg} \\\\
&=  3.7 \times 1000 \ \text {g} \\\\
&=  3700 \ \text {g}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-52E563CF-BAF0-40C4-9688-3CFD0072AE8E'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$5 \quad 6 \quad 7 \quad 10 \quad 11$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$11$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$11$

</div>
</div>

<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$11$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B1CD7CC3-1C82-46B8-9F60-E03B0C68A78A'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $4 \times (43.4 + 1.02)$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS


$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4 \times (43.4 + 1.02)  \\\\
&= 4 \times 44.42 \\\\
&= 177.68
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$177.68$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4 \times (43.4 + 1.02)  \\\\
&= 4 \times 44.42 \\\\
&= 177.68
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-0FD0289F-6CF3-4995-B86D-7A270F63D72B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

To convert kilometers to miles I multiply by $5$ and then divide by $8$.

Using this method how many miles is $240 \ \text{kilometers}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Using the given method,

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  240 \ \text {kilometers} \\\\
&=  (240 \times 5) \div 8 \ \text {miles} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {240 \times 5} {8} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {8 \times 30 \times 5} {8} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 30 \times 5} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
&=  30 \times 5 \\\\
&=  150 \ \text {miles}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$150 \ \text {miles}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using the given method,

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  240 \ \text {kilometers} \\\\
&=  (240 \times 5) \div 8 \ \text {miles} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {240 \times 5} {8} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {8 \times 30 \times 5} {8} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {\cancel {8} \times 30 \times 5} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
&=  30 \times 5 \\\\
&=  150 \ \text {miles}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$150 \ \text {miles}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-3452FC7B-6354-498B-9BE2-BF8B78CD5FC9'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What fraction of a day is $15 \ \text{minutes}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Fraction}    &= \dfrac {15 \ \text{minutes}} {1 \ \text{day}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15 \ \text{minutes}} {60 \times 24 \ \text{minutes}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15} {60 \times 24} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15} {15 \times 4 \times 24} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {15} } {\cancel {15} \times 4 \times 24} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {4 \times 24}\\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {96}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {96}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Fraction}    &= \dfrac {15 \ \text{minutes}} {1 \ \text{day}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15 \ \text{minutes}} {60 \times 24 \ \text{minutes}} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15} {60 \times 24} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {15} {15 \times 4 \times 24} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {15} } {\cancel {15} \times 4 \times 24} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {4 \times 24}\\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {96}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {1} {96}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

What is the square root of $225$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \sqrt[2]{225} \\\\
&= \sqrt[2]{5 \times 5 \times 3 \times 3} \\\\
&= 5 \times 3 \\\\
&= 15
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-89F23118-BA61-4095-8FD2-82AAA7E88489'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which of these lengths is longest?

$42\ \text{cm} \quad  0.4 \ \text{m} \quad  147 \ \text{mm} \quad  0.351 \ \text{m} \quad  33 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let's convert all the units to $\text {cm}$. 

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 42 \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.4 \ \text{m}, \quad 147 \ \text{mm}, \quad 0.351 \ \text{m}, \quad 33  \ \text{cm} \\\\
&= 42 \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.4 \times 100 \ \text{cm}, \quad \dfrac {147} {100} \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.351 \times 100 \ \text{cm}, \quad 33  \ \text{cm} \\\\
&= 42 \ \text{cm}, \quad 40 \ \text{cm}, \quad 1.47 \ \text{cm}, \quad 35.1 \ \text{cm}, \quad 33  \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Longest length $= 42 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$42 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let's convert all the units to $\text {cm}$. 

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 42 \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.4 \ \text{m}, \quad 147 \ \text{mm}, \quad 0.351 \ \text{m}, \quad 33  \ \text{cm} \\\\
&= 42 \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.4 \times 100 \ \text{cm}, \quad \dfrac {147} {100} \ \text{cm}, \quad 0.351 \times 100 \ \text{cm}, \quad 33  \ \text{cm} \\\\
&= 42 \ \text{cm}, \quad 40 \ \text{cm}, \quad 1.47 \ \text{cm}, \quad 35.1 \ \text{cm}, \quad 33  \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Longest length $= 42 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$42 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

What is $6 \times (4.1 + 1.07)$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS


$
\begin{aligned}
&= 6 \times (4.1 + 1.07)  \\\\
&= 6 \times 5.17 \\\\
&= 31.02
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$31.02$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 6 \times (4.1 + 1.07)  \\\\
&= 6 \times 5.17 \\\\
&= 31.02
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='answercodeblock'>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-68822C6B-6F06-415B-8DA6-B4B68895040B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What fraction of an hour is $4 \ \text{minutes}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Fraction}    &= \dfrac {4} {60} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {4} {4 \times 15} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {4}} {\cancel {4} \times 15} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {15}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {15}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Fraction}    &= \dfrac {4} {60} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {4} {4 \times 15} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {4}} {\cancel {4} \times 15} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {15}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {1} {15}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F49FF939-01A4-4C4C-97E5-1AF9C8DD62DC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Split $480$ into $6$ equal parts.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  \dfrac {480} {6} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {80 \times 6} {6} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {80 \times \cancel {6}} {\cancel {6}} \\\\
&=  80
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$80$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&=  \dfrac {480} {6} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {80 \times 6} {6} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {80 \times \cancel {6}} {\cancel {6}} \\\\
&=  80
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$80$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
